1736 喬山(Johnson Health)

報告日期： 2026/06/02 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 0.08 元 
喬山公告 1Q26 營收金額 117.92 億元， YoY+11.15% ，
QoQ-39.13% ，營業利益 -0.93 億元， YoY-206.5% ，
QoQ-104.89% ， 稅後盈餘0.24 億元 ，YoY-91.34% ，QoQ-98.6% ，
稅後 EPS 0.08 元。

二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.98 元 
喬山 04/2026 營收金額 39.98 億元， YoY+6.4% ，MoM-6.31% 。
預估喬山 2Q26 營收金額 133.03 億元， YoY+8.1% ，
QoQ+12.81% ， 預估營業利益10.96 億元 ，YoY+31.29% ，較 1Q26
由虧轉盈，預估稅後盈餘 6.00 億元， YoY+436.25% ，
QoQ+2409% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.98 元。

三 . 預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元，
YoY+15.98%  
商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 156 元 
觀察喬山歷史 PER 落在 8~33X，2026 年 ROE 26.7%，PER 9.1X，
評價在低檔水準，加上 2026 年營運成長，故投資建議買進，目標
價 156 元。 (12X 2026 年稅後 EPS) 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：156.0 元 
     12.0X 2026 EPS 
參考價位：118.5 元 
     9.1X 2026 EPS 
     2026/06/01 收盤

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
喬山健康科技創立於 1975 年，資本額 30.34 億元，並於 2003 年
掛牌上市，喬山為亞洲第一、世界前三大的國際專業運動健身器
材集團公司，產品包括電動跑步機、室內健身車、橢圓訓練機、
重量訓練器材、划船器材、電動按摩椅、心肺訓練器材等。喬山
以「健康、價值、共享」為企業經營理念，專注於健康科技事業
的發展，並以 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 自有品牌行銷全世界 80
餘國。生產基地位於台中、上海、與美國越南，其中台中生產高
單價的商用產品、上海主要生產製造量大的家用產品與小部分商
用產品、美國主要生產製造特殊重量訓練產品、越南主要生產製
造家用產品為主。

二.品牌定位與銷售通路  
喬山旗下擁有 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 三項自有品牌，其中
Martrix 主打高價位商用市場 ， 其主要銷售對象包括健身房 、 飯店、
運動員訓練中心、大型公司、運動專賣店等； Vision 主打中高價
位的市場，主要銷售對象以大型公司、運動專賣店為主； Horizon
主打家用市場為主，銷售對象包括運動專賣店、電商與大型運動
器材專賣店。

圖四、喬山旗下品牌定位與通路

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望

全球健身器材市場 100 億美元 (宏遠投顧推估 )，其中商用市場 50
億美元、家用市場 50 億美元。觀察全球主要廠商 2024 年營收金
額概況： Life 6.75 億美元， YoY+4.2%( 宏遠投顧推估 )；TG 7.67
億美元 ，YoY+13.6%；喬 山10.39 億美元 ，YoY+18.3%；Precor2.61
億美元， YoY+26.1% ；Pelonton 27.0 億美元， YoY-1.1% 。

預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98%

喬山商用市場客戶包括 ： 健身房北美星球健身 、 歐洲BASIC-FIT 、
拉丁美洲 Smart Fit 以及全球連鎖性健身房 Anytime Finess 、台 灣
World GYM 與健身工廠等；飯店客戶 Marriott 、STARWOOD 、
FOUR SEASONS 等。

圖五、喬山健身房客戶

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

商用市場 2025 年營收金額 12.33 億美元， YoY+18.67% 。觀察喬
山商用市場 2025 年營運成長動能包括： 1，在中國大陸健身器材
出口至美國課徵高額關稅 ， 相對於台灣與越南關稅較低，帶動健身
房客戶訂單轉向台灣與越南地區。 2，歐洲大型客戶持續拓展美國
市場，另外美國主要客戶也拓展歐洲市場，歐洲與美國主要客戶
2025 年分別成長 25~28% 與 21~24% 。3，歐洲地區受惠中小型客
戶需求增溫，帶動歐洲地成長 25~30% 。

商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

圖六、擴展商用市場新產品 /新通路

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

預估 2026 年家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元， YoY+12.68%

喬山家用市場主要以運動專賣店、電商與大型運動器材專賣店為
主。喬山為拓展家用市場零售通路，陸續在 2015 年~2016 年購併
2nd Wind 、BUSY BODY 、Leisure Fitness 與 Gym Source 等健
身器材銷售通路，喬山在北美銷售通路據點共 103 家，據點數占
北美 30% 。

家用市場 2025 年營收金額 3.55 億美元 ，YoY+17.16% 。喬 山2025
年家用營運成長主要包括： 1，BowFlex 在 2025 年貢獻營收金額
1.1~1.2 億美元， YoY+53~67% 。2，觀察歐洲地區與拉丁美洲受
惠家用健身器材營運基期較低，商用健身房興起帶動家用市場需
求熱絡，帶動歐洲地區與拉丁美洲家用市場分別成長 45~48% 與
22~25% 。3，觀察全球主要家用健身器材廠商，生產基地集中在
中國大陸，在美國對中國大陸出口產品課徵高額關稅因素，許多
訂單己移至越南生產，帶動喬山越南廠稼動率提升。

6 
家用市場 1Q26 營收金額 1.01 億美元， YoY+12.85% 。家用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，家用健身器材經歷 2020~2021 年
上半年疫情期間大好， 2021 下半年至 2023 年庫存調整， 2025 年
因關稅訂單偏保守，觀察零售通路庫存已達相對低檔水準，加上
己有廠商退出市場，將有助家用市場成長。 2，喬山持續拓展零售
據點效益顯現，例如大型量販店己向喬山採購多項家用產品線，
帶動大型量販通路營收。 3，家用旗下 BowFlex 在 2026 年推出新
產品，搶攻北美家用市場，將有助 BowFlex  2026 年出貨成長。
綜合上述，預估 2025 年喬山家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元，
YoY+12.68% 。

預估富士醫療 (含自有品牌按摩椅 )貢獻 2026 年營收金額 1.70 億
美元， YoY+6.9%

喬山 10/29/2019 公告，以 67 億日幣 （約當台幣20 億元），向「朝
日生活與健康股份有限公司 Asahi Life & Health Corporation 」買
下子公司「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI MEDICAL 
INSTRUMENT 」 60% 股權，「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI 
MEDICAL INSTRUMENT 」為日本最大按摩椅製造商，擁有業界
領先產品開發和品牌實力，另外在日本佈有綿密的銷售通路，觀
察富士醫療器年營收金額 180~200 億日幣 (約當美元 1.8~2 億美
元)，己於 03/2020 完成股權交割。喬山 12/09/2022 公告取得富
士醫療器 40% 股權 ， 交易完成後喬山持有富士醫療器100% 股權。

圖七、喬山建立全球品牌，邁向國際

富士醫療 2025 年營收金額 1.28 億美元， YoY+9.4% 。自有品牌按
摩椅 2025 年營收金額 0.31 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。觀察富士醫療營
收成長主因在於富士醫療拓展日本以外的外銷市場有成。

富士醫療 + 自有品牌按摩椅 1Q26 營收金額 0.36 億美元，
YoY+9.09% 預期 2026 年，在富士醫療持續拓展日本以外的外銷
市場，加上喬山自有品牌按摩椅穩定成長。預估富士醫療 2026 年
營收金額 1.36 億美元， YoY+6.25% ；預估喬山自有品牌按摩椅
2026 年營收金額 0.34 億元 ，YoY+9.68%， 合計兩者貢獻喬山2026
年營收金額 1.70 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

預估 2026 年稅後盈餘 39.44 億元 ，YoY+43.43% ，稅 後EPS 13.00
元

喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

公告配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8%

喬山歷年股利政策，主要以現金股利為主。回顧 2024 年，喬山股
利政策，配發現金股利 4 元，股利發放率 50% 。喬山公告 2025
年股利政策，將配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8% 。

### Page 1

產業速報  
2026 年 6 月 4 日

1 群益投顧 
聯發科 (2454 TT) 參展 Computex 202 6。 聯 發 科已 經 布 局 車 用 、Wi -Fi 8 、 AI ASIC 。聯
發科對 2026 年手機晶片業務展望保守 ， 2027 年看好 ASIC 營 收 翻 倍 成 長，營 運 正 向，
但 由 於 股 價 已 到 先 前 推 薦 目 標 價 ， 投 資 建 議 維 持Neutral 。

研 究 員 ： 莊 禮 誠  
dan.chuang@cim.capital.com.tw

聯 發 科 有 展 示 天 璣 汽 車 座 艙 平 台 C-X1 ：  
聯 發 科 有 展 示 車 用 的 解 決 方 案 C-X1 ， 合 作 對 象 包 含 輝 達(NVDA US) ，聯發
科做 CPU、 輝 達 做GPU。聯 發 科 車 用 產 品 主 要 的 競 爭 對 手 為 美 國 高 通(QCOM  
US )。C-X1 採用 3nm 製 程 ， 可 支 援 多 螢 幕 、 觸 控 螢 幕 、 支 援4K 、8K 的 顯 示
器 ， 此 解 決 方 案 主 要 是 用 於 娛 樂 系 統 ， 尚 未 整 合 通 訊 、5G 、 ADAS 功 能 。  
此 方 案 預 計 2026 年 開 始 貢 獻 營 收 ， 第 一 個 客 戶 為 中 國 客 戶 ， 未 來 有 機 會 打
入歐 、 美 、 日 、 韓 ... 等車廠 ， 聯 發 科 的 優 勢包含 與 輝 達 的 關 係 良 好 。

圖 片 來 源 ： Mon eyDJ 、 聯 發 科 。  
AI 有 應 用 於 網 通 產 品：  
聯 發 科 有 展 示 AI 加上 Wi -Fi 的 解 決 方 案 。應用 AI， 在 網 路 出 問 題 的 時 候 可
以 不 用 派 人 到 府 解 決 ， 此 晶 片 會 整 合 進 Router /Gateway 裡 面 ， 目 前 此 產 品
還在 概 念 驗 證(Proof of Concept ， POC) 階段 ， 一 開 始 會 先 導 入 企 業 ， 而 非 個
人用戶。

產業速報  
2026 年 6 月 4 日

2 群益投顧

圖 片 來 源 ： 聯 發 科 。  
AI 也 可 用 於 節 能 ， 在 用 戶 的 使 用 量 沒 有 那 麼 大 的 時 候 ，AI 可 以 把 產 品 的 功
率從 8W 下降 50% 至 4W，目 前 此 產 品 還 在 POC 階段 ，未 來 可 能 的 客 戶 包 含
歐 洲 運 營 商 。

3 群益投顧

圖 片 來 源 ： 聯 發 科 。  
聯 發 科 已 經 有 佈 局 Wi -Fi 8 ：  
聯發科的 Premium  Wi -Fi  8 晶 片 會 使 用 6nm 的 製 程 ， 雖 然 Wi -Fi  7 的全球滲
透 率 還 不 高，對 於 一 些 南 美 國 家，可 能 2026 年 才 開 始 導 入 Wi -Fi 7 的 產 品，
Wi -Fi 8 的 產 品 可 能 要 等 到 2H27~2028 的 滲 透 率 才 會 有 較 顯 著 的 增 加。

圖 片 來 源 ： 聯 發 科 。  
聯 發 科 有將 Micro LED 應用於 資 料 中 心 的 傳輸 ：  
聯 發 科 有 展 示 Micro LED ，應 用 於 資 料 中 心 傳 輸 的 解 決 方 案， 合 作 對 象 是 微
軟 研 究 院 。 用 Micro LED 的 優 點 是 功 耗與 成 本 較 傳 統 雷 射 來 的 低； 缺 點 是
傳 輸 距 離 較 短， 此 產 品 尚 未 量 產。 聯 發 科 是 設 計 晶 片 ， 用 的 不 是 先 進 製 程 ，
晶 片 會 放 在 Cable 裡 面 。

### Page 5

產業速報

5 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (StrongBuy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (TradingBuy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
E： (Environmental) 。盟立積極推動低碳永續轉型，將環境責任視為公司運
作中的重要任務之一。透過宣導並實踐節電、節水及廢棄物分類等措施，在
提升資源使用效率的同時，將對環境的影響降到最低。盟立不僅在內部營運
中積極落實環保措施，更致力於影響並引導供應鏈及客戶共同參與環境保
護。  
S：(Social) 。盟立公司持續強化其網站和社群平台的運營，並且新增多個新
產品頁面，以適應市場需求。特別是針對 2024 年最熱門的技術領域，如
FOPLP 、EFEM 、Omiverse 、浸沒式散熱技術和 AI 機器人等，盟立持續更新
中英文網站頁面，並將其打造成熱門的產品展示頁面。通過網站和社群平台
的積極運營，盟立進一步加強了與客戶和業界的聯繫，提升了品牌的網路知
名度與市場影響力。  
G：(Governance) 。盟立鼓勵股東參與公司治理，除了參加股東會，亦積極了
解股東的意見和關注的議題，並明確解釋公司的政策，以獲得股東的支持。

3 群益投顧 
盟立 2026 年將有顯著擴產：  
盟立 2026 年整體產能擴增 30%~40% ，藉由全球生產基地及健全供應鏈，快
速回應客戶需求。半導體高端設備產能將擴增超過 30% 、機器人 /機器狗及
關節模組供應鏈產能將會達到 500 台 /年。  
盟立 2026 年半導體設備系統合併營收比重可望達到 60% 以上：  
盟立表示，東南亞（馬來西亞、新加坡、泰國）半導體客戶案件推動順暢，
規劃中之案件預計 2Q26 取得訂單並陸續出貨。 2026 年半導體設備系統合
併營收比重可望達到 60% 以上。  
根據半導體、物流及製造業客戶的實際場域情況和需求，訂定 AI 人形機器
人與機器狗的規格及功能。盟立結合集團力量，推動本體的開發。 AI 機器
人及機器狗的本體開發已於 3Q25 完成，預計 1Q26 開始出貨。  
智能自動化物流需求已經回溫：  
智能自動化物流部分，美國關稅政策影響趨於明朗，客戶已陸續確立投資設
廠決策，預期 2025 年因不確定因素而延宕的自動化系統案件將逐漸回溫，
為 2026 年挹注營運動能。目前來自北美、歐洲及東南亞的需求已逐漸確定，
已承接 EMS 客戶北美廠自動化系統訂單， 2026 年出貨。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

M
Update
Aspeed Technology  | Asia Pacific
Initiating Another Round of
Price Hike in 2H26; OW
Key takeaways from today's Aspeed 1Q26 earnings call:
• 3Q26 guidance update: Aspeed guides 3Q revenue to NT$4.1-4.3bn, inline
with MSe of NT$4.23bn and above consensus of NT$3.75bn. GM guidance:
67-68% vs. consensus 68%.
• Qualifying more OSAT to support output: Substrate and OSAT remain key
constraints. 3Q substrate supply might be 2-3mn/month, with output rising

to 3mn/month and above once E-glass helps. Besides SPIL, Aspeed is adding
another Taiwanese OSAT to support increasing wafer output in 4Q. As
material and packaging costs rise, we expect Aspeed to initiate another price
hike, supporting GM to reach our expected 70% in 2H26.
• New product ramp: AST2700/2750 will start ramping in 2H26, AST1040
(next-gen SMC) will be production ready in 1H27, and AST1840 (eFPGA SoC)
will start ramping in 2H27.
Stay OW: We believe management is guiding 3Q GM conservatively, but we do see a

slight margin impact from the substrate price hike. We still expect margin to reach
70% after a potential second round of price hike in 4Q26  (see our Lattice
partnership note
).

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Nanya Technology Corp.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
DRAM supply/demand and pricing outlook: Nanya thinks restoring the supply and
demand balance will keep taking more time as demand continues to strengthen.
New capacity ramps will remain limited until 2028. Although management expects
pricing to continue rising in 3Q26, the increase will not match prior quarters.
DRAM product portfolio:  Although DDR4 is Nanya Tech’s current focus, it said it

can flexibly choose the production node (LPDDR4/5 and DDR5) and dynamically
adjust supply to meet customer demand. 16nm is the mainstream solution now but
it will continue migrating to 14/15nm.
LTA: As it still cannot fulfill existing customer demand, Nanya Tech said it is not
seeking to  sign further long-term agreements
.
WoW and SiCap: It has started to see initial wafer on wafer (WoW) revenue, but
large-scale contribution might take 1-2 years once new capacity is ready in 2027-28.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Nanya Technology Corp. (2408.TW)
Base case, P/B. We expect the stock to trade at 3.22x 2026, 1.88x 2027, and 1.53x 2028 BVPS,
above its average since 2015. We think NTC will benefit from a supply-driven up-cycle as major
memory players are exiting the DDR4 market. That should help NTC more than offset the
competitive impact from CXMT in the near term.
Risks to Upside
n Sustained DRAM prices from more disciplined supply and stronger demand

n Stronger pricing environment, enabling revenue guidance to be beaten
n Faster-than-expected 1a/1b nm ramp-up progress
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected 1a/1b nm ramp-up
n Less demand for specialty DRAM from 4K2K TVs and smart set-top boxes

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Mitac (3706.TW): Computex 2026: liquid-cooled AI server rack
showcased; AI turnkey solution facilitating AI deployment; Buy
2 June 2026 | 11:20PM HKT
We visited Mitac’s booth on June 2 at our Computex tour in Taipei. Mitac showcased
its diversified AI infr astructure products, such as liquid and air-cooled AI server
racks, scalable PCIe GPU servers, storage rack, and AI deployment turnkey solutions.
Management holds on a positive view on AI application and infrastructure end

demand and highlighted that Mitac’s turnkey solution could support clients’
high-efficienc y AI deployment. We are positive on Mitac riding on the diversified
chipset platform trend in AI servers, which provides customers with higher
customization, flexibility , while reducing reliance on single chipset supplier.
Key takeaways: Mitac showcased its diversified r ack-level products at Computex
(link), including the 52U high-density AI liquid-cooled rack, which maximizes the

compute per footprint, saves space, and delivers strong thermal performance. Mitac
offers f ull stack AI turnkey solutions and AI factory solutions, covering the whole AI
deployment process across AI infrastructure management, intelligent AI data
platform, security and OS, and diversified AI ser ver models, enabling the
high-efficienc y AI deployment for enterprise clients. Management remains positive
on the AI application and infrastructure end demand, and Mitac’s accumulated

experiences in AI server manufacturing and turnkey solution for AI deployment
should enable the company to capture the end demand and support the company’s
growth.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

M
Update
AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
May Sales -10% MoM/+40%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its recent share price movement.
What's new:
• May sales came in at NT$3,909mn (-10% MoM/+40% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to the fewer working days amid the May
holidays in China.
• By segment, shipments from battery, machine tool, general machinery and
textiles  outperformed during the month.

• QTD revenue reached 73% of MSe at NT$11,342mn (+13% QoQ/+27% YoY)
and 75% of consensus at NT$10,941mn (+9% QoQ/+22% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 2Q26 revenue tracking ahead of expectations.
•  Orders for the month continued to exceed shipments in April, and both are
above its own expectations.
• Management indicated that its operating margin has reached a record high in
April and could continue to expand. We do not have its monthly history, but
its record-high single-quarter operating margin was 35.3% in 2Q10, so It

implies it has surpassed the above level in April on a single month basis (vs.
2Q26 consensus at 34.5%).
• It believes that the impact from rising material costs should be manageable
owing to its raised selling prices in the overseas market and efficiency/
product mix improvement.
• We stay OW on AirTAC amid the industry upcycle and its consistent share
gains. Moreover, current valuation of 23x 2027e P/E seems undemanding vs.
its mid-cycle average of 25x, in our view.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along with
business cycles). As the industry enters an upcycle, we think applying a peak cycle valuation
of 30x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Taiwan Semiconductors and Electronic Components &

03 June 2026 Citi Research
2
800V HVDC: From Server Power Upgrade to Full AI Factory Architecture Shift  —
On the power system, the shift to 800V HVDC is not just a PSU upgrade, but a full
AI rack architecture change. Traditional AC systems require multiple conversion
stages from UPS, distribution, server PSU and low-voltage DC delivery, causing
power loss, heat, copper weight and cabling complexity. 800V HVDC reduces

conversion steps and brings high-voltage DC closer to compute racks, supporting
future rack density from around 100kW toward 500kW–1MW. This changes the
supplier’s opportunity. Busbar, cable and connector vendors benefit from higher
demand for compact high-voltage DC busbars, 125A+ connectors, hot-swap
design, insulation, arcing protection and rack-level safety. PSU suppliers move
from server PSU modules toward centralized power shelves and power racks

integrating rectifiers, DC/DC conversion, BBU, monitoring and protection. Cooling
also becomes more critical: higher rack power makes liquid cooling mandatory,
driving CDU, cold plates, manifolds, leak detection and quick-disconnect demand.
Overall, 800V HVDC turns AI infrastructure from server assembly into power-
cooling-compute system integration, favoring suppliers with system design,
reliability and safety certification capability. We are positive on Hon Hai, Delta and

AVC.
ASPEED: From BMC to Server-Control Platform —  Global No. 1 BMC maker
ASPEED also demonstrates its product roadmap and is moving from a traditional
BMC upgrade cycle toward a broader server-control platform for AI servers. The
core BMC transition is AST2700/2750, which starts ramping in 2H26, followed by
AST2800, targeted for mass production in 2028. AST2800 carries roughly 2x the
cost of AST2700, implying higher ASP , specifications, more advanced process

adoption and stronger content per server. Beyond BMC, ASPEED is expanding into
SMC, PRo T, I/O expander and eFPGA SoC products. AST1040, the next-generation
SMC, is expected to be production-ready in 1H27, while AST1840, the eFPGA SoC,
should ramp in 2H27. The company also plans to integrate Caliptra into new
generations of all chips, highlighting the rising importance of server security,
platform root-of-trust and remote-management functions. The bigger trend is

that AI servers require more telemetry, security, control and firmware-level
coordination, making BMC/SMC less like low-value support chips and more like
essential infrastructure controllers.  T ogether with various racks including existing
NVL72 and ASIC business, the new CPU, LPU, storage and SPX racks from Nvidia,
we expect strong BMC growth in the longer term to persist.

Asia Vital Components
(3017.TW; NT$2700.0; 1; 02 Jun 26; 13:30)
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$3,450 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified
given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,

rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-
plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not

been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.
Delta Electronics
(2308.TW; NT$2360.0; 1; 02 Jun 26; 13:30)
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$2,480 is based on 40x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since

2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with
customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly

than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.
Hon Hai Precision
(2317.TW; NT$301.5; 1; 02 Jun 26; 13:30)
Valuation
Our target price of NT$360 is based on 20x 2026E EPS, which is at the higher
end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is justified by the
company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook, as well as new

endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon Hai has been
able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe a P/E
valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

and 4) higher-than-expected impact of COVID-19 or other macro factors.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

## Document 093: 260603_gs_GCE

Source PDF: all report pdf/260603_gs_GCE.pdf

### Page 1

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei
Branch
GCE (2368.TW): Computex & Corporate Day: New ASIC project win with
meaningful upside driven by the large TAM; Buy
3 June 2026 | 3:40AM CST
We hosted meetings with GCE at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day (June
2nd). Key takeaways include:
(1) The company has announced a new ASIC project win from a major CSP
customer for the firs t time, with shipment expected to begin in 4Q26. The project is
expected to carry higher ASP and GM, driven by higher PCB spec of 30+ layer count.

(2) The company is seeing strong new order momentum in 400G/800G switches,
particularly from a major CSP customer, supporting near-term demand visibility.
(3) The company is currently in the R&D phase for 1.6T switches and expect small
volume production starting in 2026 end. The PCB spec for 1.6T switches is 46-52
layers, upgraded to M9 grade CCL with low-DK2 glass fiber. Management mentioned
that MLB PCB will remain the mainstream solution for switches for the next two

years, meanwhile sees some customers shifting toward HDI thick board solutions in
2028.
(4) The company expects its GM to continue to go up sequentially throughout 2026,
while mentioning FX as the main near-term headwind.
(5) For the Thailand plant, the company believes the average monthly capacity value
could reach NT$1.3bn in 3Q26 (vs. NT$600mn in 2Q26) mainly driven by the ramp up
of phase 2, which is the main reason that overall average monthly capacity value will

reach NT$8.4bn in 3Q26 (vs. NT$7.5bn in 2Q26).
(6) To meet with the strong AI-demand, the company is being proactive with its
capacity expansion plan, including three new plants over the next three years:
Thailand 2nd plant in 2027, Suzhou 2nd plant in 2028 and a new Taiwan plant in
2029.
Investment view
We maintain our positive view on GCE following firs t announcement of a new ASIC
project win from a major CSP customer, with shipment expected to begin in 4Q26.

While we have not estimated the market share yet, based on specs shared by the
company on the project and our GC Tech teams AI PCB model forecasts, we think the
PCB TAM for this specific pr oject could be ~USD$10bn, or equivalent to ~1.7x of
GCE’s 2027E revenue. We see meaningful upside potential as the project features
higher PCB spec (30+ layer counts), which should support both revenue growth and
GM expansion. Based on our estimation, GCE’s AI projects average GM level is now at

www.gs.com/research/hedge.html
. Analysts employed by non-US affiliates are not registered/qualified as research
analysts with FINRA in the U.S.
Equity Research
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 2

40-50% (with more upside potential driven by better pricing outlook or better
production yield), comparing to the company’s average level of ~35% now. The
increasing AI server revenue contribution also suggests a much more favorable GM
outlook in coming quarters, which is in line with management comments that GM will go
up sequentially throughout 2026.
GCE is a key cloud (server+switch) PCB supplier globally with 20%+ market share in
2022. We expect cloud PCB market demand to grow at a 15% 2024-26E CAGR (far

stronger than the overall RPCB+HDI market demand growth rate at 5% 2024-26E
CAGR). We believe GCE’s proactive capacity expansion plan (33% in 2026) and high
annualized ROI (300%+) will continue to drive the company’s revenue/margin level in the
coming quarters/years. Additionally, we believe GCE can continue to see strong demand
from the increasing content per server/switch, as we expect both markets to see a
20-50%+ layer count increase after the generation upgrade (upgrade to 400G/Eagle

Stream). We are constructive on GCE’s long-term growth opportunity given its solid
position in the rapidly growing cloud PCB market, and we are Buy rated on the stock.
GCE is trading higher than its historical P/E average level driven by strong AI server
demand, but lower than the TW AI server players’ average.
Price Target Risks and Methodology
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,585 is based on a 22x 4Q26E-3Q27E P/E
(in-line with the AI PCB leaders valuation in the past 3 years).

Key downside risks: (1) weaker-than-expected AI server market demand; (2) delays in
the company’s Nvidia OAM/UBB product qualifica tion, (3) weaker-than-expected HDI
demand, and (4) higher-than-expected ramp up cost for new capacity

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

by Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research and referred to in this research.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei

Lotes (3533.TW): Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day: Server remains the
key growth driver , despite ongoing material cost & supply risk
3 June 2026 | 6:52PM CST
We hosted meetings with Lotes at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day (June
2nd). Key takeaways include:
(1) Management expects CPU-related product shipments to grow ~20% YoY in 2026,
after factoring in potential material/component supply shortage, while key

shortages include CPU, memory, PCB, and passive components.
(2) Lotes continues to make progress in new AI-related products and expects to
begin shipping SoCAMM in 3Q26 (Lotes is the first supplier for Vera Rubin), and also
expects NPO products to start contributing in 2027. Management expects AI-related
products to account for 2-3% of total revenue in 2026, with contribution expanding
to double-digit over the next 3-5 years.
(3) Lotes expect raw material cost pressure on GM to persist into 2Q26. However,

management expects GM to improve in 2H26, driven by the release of new AMD
Venice CPU, which will see ASP increase of at least 15% vs. previous generation (23%
increase in pin count vs. AMD’s SP5 socket).
(4) The company continues to engage in price negotiations with clients, while pricing
for AMD Venice has already incorporated higher material costs. Management
mentioned the base case is no price down for the existing and new products (vs.
typical industry practice with price erosion of ~5% in year 1, ~3% in year 2 and ~1%

in year 3 after new product release).
(5) For the CPU platform, Lotes mentioned Eagle Stream penetration increased from
75-80% in 1Q26 to current 80-85%, while Birch Stream should further increase to
20-30% by end of 2026 from the current 15%. For AMD, Venice should account for
15-20% (revise down from 20-25%) of total servers by the end of 2026 but with
majority of contribution from high-end solution (SP7 with 7536 pins) and expects to
see contribution from the low-end solution (SP8 with 5572 pins) starting in 2027.

Investment view
We maintain our relative cautious view on Lotes’ business outlook, considering (1)
the rising material cost environment might sustain and the company has adopted a
stable pricing rather than cost pass through via price increase, given the risk of losing
market share and (2) limited contribution from new AI-related products (including
SoCAMM, QD, GPU socket and CPO/NPO socket). We continue to see strong CPU
server demand in near term and extend to coming years (see AMD report here

www.gs.com/research/hedge.html

which we believe will be a key growth driver for Lotes in near-to-mid term; however, we
continue to see risk of rising material costs, which should offset the margin expansion
driven by new CPU socket products.
Lotes is a connector supplier mainly focusing on the niche CPU socket market with more
than 40% share in 2024, but we see pressure for its market share outlook in the coming
years considering the intensifying competition in the server CPU market. We maintain

our positive view on the company’s GM expansion potential longer term, which we
believe will be mainly driven by better product mix (Venice server CPU to be released in
2H26 and Oak Stream server CPU to be released in 2027, both with better ASP and
margin). Meanwhile, Lotes’ CPU socket product line’s ASP/GM are higher than the
company average, and we see scope for both to grow even faster in the new product
generation given the larger socket size and pin count required. However, the company’s

new product line, Quick Disconnects (UQD or MQD) for liquid cooling systems, is likely to
deliver very limited revenue/gross profit c
ontribution in the coming years (0.4/1.0%

product pricing adjustment; (3) slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related
contribution

2H26E-1H27E EPS.

slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related contribution

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

• RTX Spark: Our checks suggest the RTX Spark NB targets to start shipping
in fall 2026, with ASP of >US$3k and combined shipments of N1 and N1X
"targeted" for  5-10mn in the first 12 months.
• "New" QD: Our checks suggest NVIDIA is looking to introduce a new design

for QDs used in the Rubin Ultra rack systems.
• Midplane connector: Based on our checks, FIT also got licensed for Paladin
HD2 connectors for midplane and will likely be the key partner for the next-
gen connector, though details around its designs have not been finalized.
• BlueField®-4 STX storage rack:
One tray contains 2 Vera, 4 BF-4 and 24
SSDs, in 2U form factor. There will be 16 trays in one STX rack; each tray
should consume 1.2-1.3kW. Our checks also suggest 1 STX rack will be paired

with 8 VR200 NVL72 racks.
• Spectrum X CPO switch:
Wistron showcased a Spectrum X CPO switch in
collaboration with NVIDIA. The CPO switch board PCB might use M6 CCLs
only.
• ELSFP:
FIT showcases its ELSFP products  in 20dBm (decibel-
milliwatts)/100mW (milliwatts) and 23dBm/200mW. Our checks suggest the
ASP of a 20dBm ELSFP could be close to ~US$500.

Key Takeaways
We don't think Kyber will be ready for mass deployment in Rubin Ultra.
All R200 HGX servers will be liquid-cooled, in 2U  form factor. Each server rack
will be able to house 9 nodes at most.
Groq 3 LPX rack: One tray contains 1 Intel CPU, 2 FPGA, 16 LPX and 28 QDs, with
double-sided main PCB,  which is 52L (26+26).
Vera CPU rack: A NVIDIA MGX Vera CPU board contains 2 Vera and 1.5TB

SOCAMM memory  (96GB x16) per board. Each tray houses 4 CPU boards (so 8
Vera per tray).
800V DC power rack: Our checks suggest the standalone 800V DC power rack is
on track to ship in 4Q26.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 099: 260604_daiwa_Taiwan Datacentre Hardware

Source PDF: all report pdf/260604_daiwa_Taiwan Datacentre Hardware.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-04
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: During Computex 2026, we arranged booth tours for our investors with 
representatives of companies within our coverage, including Wiwynn, Aspeed, Delta, Hon 
Hai, and Chenbro, alongside several other companies. In conjunction with keynote 
addresses delivered by leading global technology companies, we believe the primary focus 
of Computex 2026 remained centered on the CPU domain - specifically, the role of CPUs 
in orchestrating the broader ecosystem for agentic AI, as well as the emerging total 
addressable market (TAM) arising from AI PCs incorporating NVIDIA's Spark chipset. 
Across the booths we visited, the majority of vendors showcased components and 
solutions applicable to NVIDIA's Vera Rubin generation platform. These demonstrations 
should be regarded as indicative of trial production activity within the Taiwan supply chain 
ahead of the official volume ramp, which is anticipated to commence in 4Q26, with the 
underlying concepts having been primarily introduced at GTC. With respect to key 
technology transitions, the developments that garnered the most attention at the 
conference included CPO-based AI server architecture, Co-Packaging Connectors (CPC), 
High-Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) power delivery, and microgrid solutions. In conclusion, 
we encourage investors to maintain a sustained focus on CPU-related investment themes, 
as well as on specification upgrades driven primarily by escalating high-speed 
transmission requirements. Our key Buy ideas include: Aspeed, Lotes (3533 TT, 
TWD2,400), Wiwynn, Delta, TUC (6274 TT, TWD1,695), EMC (2383 TT, TWD4,830), and 
Accton (2345 TT, TWD2,510).

Wiwynn (6669 TT, TWD5,570, Buy [1]) 
• According to investor feedback, Wiwynn's booth was widely regarded as one of the 
most compelling exhibitions at Computex, owing to its demonstration of a Co-Packaged 
Optics (CPO) computing rack. The demonstration was developed in collaboration with 
several key partners, including Ayar Labs - contributing the optical engine and External 
Laser Small Form Factor Pluggable (ELSFP) technology - and GUC (3443 TT 
TWD4,400, Hold [3], covered by Rick Hsu), contributing the XPU. It is our understanding 
that the demonstration remains under development, and the collaboration with Ayar 
Labs is currently in a pilot stage. Wiwynn's objective is to ensure that it acquires the 
requisite capabilities and expertise ahead of CPO technology reaching broader 
commercial maturity. Based on our observations, Wiwynn's focus appears to be 
concentrated on computing tray development, while the scale-out and scale-up switch 
racks are expected to be contributed by other partners. 
• Another notable highlight from Wiwynn's exhibition was its demonstration of AMD's 
Helios platform. Based on our understanding, AMD's Helios architecture would comprise 
18 computing units, each configured with four GPUs and one CPU per rack. Given the 
physical dimensions of AMD's MI450 series GPU and connectivity speed requirements, 
the GPU layout is positioned at the bottom of the PCB in order to optimize connectivity 
with the scale-up system. According to Wiwynn, the Helios platform will leverage UALink 
for scale-up interconnects, while adopting Ethernet for scale-out connectivity 
(implementing UALink over Ethernet).

Aspeed (5274 TT, TWD18,265, Buy [1]) 
• At Computex 2026, Aspeed's showcase highlighted its evolution from a pure Baseboard 
Management Controller (BMC) supplier toward a broader platform management and

infrastructure control provider. The company demonstrated solutions spanning BMC, 
Satellite Management Controller (SMC, or mini-BMC), I/O expansion, and security IC. 
• A key emphasis at Aspeed's booth was the company's adoption of RISC-V architecture 
and OCP open-source frameworks to support Caliptra — an IP designed to execute 
root-of-trust functions. This security enhancement is expected to be implemented across 
the majority of Aspeed's product lines, thereby elevating the overall security standard of 
its devices. 
• Another significant highlight was the introduction of the AST1840, succeeding the 
previous AST1800. The AST1700/1800/1840 series constitutes Aspeed's I/O expander 
product line, with the AST1800 marking the first edition to incorporate an embedded 
FPGA within the I/O expander. With the AST1840, Aspeed has transitioned to 
outsourcing the FPGA chip from Lattice Semiconductor, resulting in the development of 
a new System-on-Chip (SoC) for the AST1840. 
• Regarding the near-term outlook, following the company's revenue guidance of 
TWD4.1-4.3 billion, we anticipate an additional revenue step-up from 4Q26, as Aspeed 
has qualified with a second OSAT partner, enabling broader support from substrate 
vendors. With respect to the T-glass to E-glass substrate certification, we expect the 
transition to be completed successfully; however, the initial production batch of the 
AST2700 will incorporate substrates containing both T-glass and E-glass. Over the 
longer term, only the E-glass substrate version is expected to remain in production. 
• The company expressed a continued positive outlook on CPU server demand, driven by 
the growth of agentic AI workloads. According to Aspeed's latest forecast, BMC unit 
shipments are projected to reach approximately 8.5 million units in 2026. Looking ahead 
to 2027, we think BMC shipments from the conventional server segment will grow by at 
least 30% year-over-year.

Delta Electronics (2308 TT, TWD2,425, Buy [1]) 
• Delta’s Computex 2026 showcase its strategy of extending from power and thermal 
components into broader AI datacentre infrastructure. Delta highlighted five main 
exhibition areas: modular datacentre, micro grid solutions, HVDC solutions, Vera Rubin 
solutions, and Edge AI. We believe the key investment implication is that Delta is 
positioning itself not only as a supplier of power supplies and cooling components, but 
also as an infrastructure partner spanning power conversion. 
• 800VDC remains the core product direction. Delta’s key AI datacentre message is built 
around 800VDC architecture. Its showcase included an 800VDC in-row power system, 
90kW DC-DC power shelf, 110kW AC-DC power shelf, 12kW power distribution board, 
and DC-DC conversion modules. The 800VDC in-row power system is designed to 
simplify the power conversion architecture and achieve over 98% system efficiency, 
while also integrating 480kW of total BBU for short-duration support to critical loads. We 
see this as the next step in Delta’s power roadmap as AI rack power emerge. 
• Micro-grid is becoming the next key question from investors on top of HVDC, while SST 
and SOFC becomes the key discussion. However, these new growth drivers likely won’t 
bear fruit in just 1-2 years. The microgrid solution integrates renewables, energy 
storage, fuel cells and other power sources to support datacentre operation in grid-
connected and off-grid modes. Delta’s solid-state transformer (SST) can directly convert 
medium-voltage AC to low-voltage 800VDC, with high power density and modular 
design to reduce footprint and power losses. The SOFC fuel cell offers 60% generation 
efficiency and 24-hour stable power supply with modular expansion. We see SST and 
SOFC as strategic long-term options tied to AI datacentre power trend, while the nearer-
term investment focus remains 800VDC power racks, power shelves, distribution 
boards, BBU, and liquid cooling.

### Page 3

Taiwan Datacentre Hardware 
4 June 2026 
3

Hon Hai (2317 TT, TWD293.0, Buy [1]) 
• As one of NVIDIA's key strategic partners, Hon Hai presented its comprehensive lineup 
of NVIDIA AI servers at Computex. Notably, the Vera CPU rack and CPO switch were 
absent from the exhibition. Hon Hai's demonstration included new computing trays and 
switches for the VR200, as well as the HGX NVL8 — a new HGX configuration featuring 
eight GPUs, dual x86 CPUs, and integrated liquid-cooling components. The compact 
design of the NVL8 enables a reduced form factor of 2U (with a 1U height of 45mm). 
• In addition, Hon Hai showcased its Co-Packaging Connector (CPC), designed for 
integration into switches such as the Spectrum-X. Based on our understanding, Hon 
Hai/FIT is capable of delivering 224 Gbps per lane, achieving an aggregate throughput 
of 12.8 Tbps per connector. We believe that at least two clients are currently evaluating 
the adoption of this CPC technology.

• From FIT's perspective, the company also presented its Paladin connector, intended for 
use within the mid-plane of the computing tray. This connector, along with the mid-plane, 
is expected to serve as a key replacement for Amphenol's flyover cable. We believe FIT 
is actively developing a next-generation connector solution to be deployed within the 
Kyber architecture, where the mid-plane will serve as the interconnect between both the 
computing and switch trays.

Chenbro (8210 TT, TWD1,420, Buy [1]) 
• As an established rack vendor on NVIDIA's Reference Vendor List (RVL), Chenbro 
showcased several rack solutions compatible with NVIDIA's VR NVL72 at the exhibition. 
• We understand that Chenbro is currently engaged in multiple new rack projects, 
encompassing noise-cancellation chassis, IT racks for NVIDIA/AMD, liquid-cooling racks 
(including CDU racks in both Liquid-to-Liquid and Liquid-to-Air configurations), and 
switch racks. This strategic expansion underscores Chenbro's transition from a chassis 
vendor to a full-scale rack vendor, reflecting the broader industry shift whereby end 
customers are increasingly designing their infrastructure at the rack level rather than the 
chassis level. 
• We anticipate that the margin profile for rack solutions will reach c.25-30%, compared to 
our previous estimate of c.15-20%, driven by the significant degree of customization 
involved. Consequently, our revised long-term gross margin forecast has been lifted 
from 25-26% to 28-29%.  
• During the booth tour, Chenbro emphasized its enhanced development cycle, which is 
designed to further reduce time-to-market for its clients. The company also highlighted 
its diversified development teams - comprising software engineers, industrial designers, 
mechanical engineers, and other specialists - to provide comprehensive support to end 
customers. We believe Chenbro's role is a key co-design and the most fast partner for 
its end customers, making it a critical differentiator from its competitors and mitigating 
the impact of competitive pressures.

infrastructure investments, and the customization / diversifica tion trend (e.g. HGX,

industrial PC market, expanding into the Edge AI market. The company provides
Flash Storage, DRAM Module as parts of its traditional business, GPU and ASIC
solutions for global leading AI infrastructure customers, and also software, sensor,
camera and other products in Edge AI market. Currently, the company operates

branches in over 25 regions globally, with an R&D center and manufacturing center in
its Taiwan headquarter.

2026, reflec

enhance clients’ operational efficienc

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

### Page 9

Earnings revision and where we stand vs. consensus
Earnings revisions
We revise up our 2026/27/28E EPS by 1/5/24% to factor in higher ASP per watt for its AI
PSU business and higher penetration for its power rack business (we raise up revenue
estimates by 2/23% for 2027/28E). For the HVDC solution, we expect demand in late
2026 with faster progress from the +-400VDC solutions, and continue to expect
stronger HVDC demand in 2027E/2028E. Therefore, we revise up our 2026/27/28E GM

by 0.1/0.5/0.1ppt due to the slight margin expansion driven by better scale.
Where are we vs. consensus?
Our 2026/27/28E revenue estimates are 7/20/47% higher than Bloomberg consensus,
due to our positive view on the AI power related business opportunity (we believe AI
power revenue, including AC/DC, DC/DC and power rack products, will reach
NT$156bn/NT$486bn/NT$1,434bn in 2026/27/28E). As such, considering our high
estimate for Delta’s AI power product GM at 50%, our 2026/27/28E GM estimate is

3.3/6.1/6.0ppt higher than Bloomberg consensus, as we believe the company’s revenue
will be mainly driven by AI power products in the coming years (20%/41%/66% revenue
contribution in 2026/27/28E).
As shown in Exhibit 17, our GM estimates for Delta in 2027/28 are much higher than
Bloomberg consensus, due to our more positive view on the GM expansion driven by AI
server/datacenter power related products, which should continue to see increasing
contribution to revenue in the coming years. We believe the market has been too

conservative on estimating Delta’s GM potential, given the fact that Delta has proven
that its AI product revenue/GM is much higher than company average (GM improved

### Page 11

Valuation and risks - Raise TP to NT$4,500
We reiterate our Buy rating on Delta with a new TP of NT$4,500 (from NT$2,420),
implying 96% upside. We believe the company will continue to deliver a solid earnings
growth rate (100%+ 2025-28E EPS CAGR) which is much faster than the company’s
historical average level (~10% CAGR in the past 20 years) and AI power industry peers
(15% CAGR).
Our 12-month target price is based on an unchanged 26.5x P/E, and we roll over our

valuation base from 2Q27-1Q28E to 2028E, discounted back to 2027 at a CoE of 11%,
given the much improved visibility on HVDC power rack adoption timeline. The 26.5x P/E
multiple is +1.6x STDC higher than Delta’s past 15-year average valuation, and we
believe Delta, as the key AI power product supplier with a solid market position (50%+
market share in coming years on GSe) should at least trade above historical valuation.
Our TP-implied 22.0x 2027-28E P/B is much higher than the company’s average P/B

multiple in the past (Exhibit 21); however, after considering the high ROE (increasing to
39%/58%/87% in 2026/27/28E based on GSe), we believe this higher implied multiple is
justified as the c ompany should be traded at a premium considering its accelerating
expansion in ROE in coming years.

### Page 12

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks
Delta Electronics is the largest vendor of power supply components globally (in terms of
sales) and a key servo motor/inverter/PLC supplier in mainland China. It is also a key
supplier for the EV/server power industry, holding one of the largest market shares in
both markets. We are positive on Delta’s long-term growth opportunity driven by
multiple factors (EV/EV charger/5G infrastructure/Datacenter power/ESS etc.) in the

coming years. We also expect demand for its AI server power and cooling systems to
deliver solid growth given the increasing demand for high computing performance AI
servers, which will require higher efficienc
y power components and better cooling
systems to improve the overall performance. Trading at a 1-year forward P/E multiple
that is below our target multiple, we believe the stock is undervalued, given the
company’s strong revenue outlook (50%+ 2025-28E CAGR) for its growth sectors

(including EV & EV charging / green capex & renewable energy / cloud computing /
smart manufacturing / satellite communication & smart connectivity), and its leadership
in the power component industry. We are Buy rated. Key downside risks include: (1)
slower-than-expected AI server power consumption growth momentum, (2) potential
market share loss risk in AI server DC-DC power system or the potential design change
on AI server DC-DC system, and (3) slower than expected of future products

deployment, including power rack, SST and SOFC.
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Delta. Our 12m TP of NT$4,500 is based
on a target P/E multiple of 26.5x (1.6x s.d. higher than Delta’s past 15-year average
valuation) applied to our 2028E EPS and discounted back to 2027 at a CoE of 11%.
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected AI server power consumption growth
momentum, (2) potential market share loss risk in AI server DC-DC power system or the

potential design change on AI server DC-DC system, and (3) slower than expected of
future products deployment, including power rack, SST and SOFC.
Exhibit 21: Delta is trading at record high on P/B,
supported by a solid ROE uptrend
Exhibit 22: The high P/B is still below industry linear
regression (vs. ROE), and we expect the company will
deliver a solid ROE outlook in coming years

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,

Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The
normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a

higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial
stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .
